the onset $z_0$, so ``kink-slope value $+$ sign'' is an equivalent atom (slope
$\leftrightarrow$ onset is a bijection, mirroring $\rho(z_c)=\ln2\iff
z_c=\sqrt3/2$). Hence ``D4 is \emph{the} intended minimal radius axiomatization''
is \tagC{} (candidate) --- a modeling choice, not a forced uniqueness.
\end{remark}

\begin{proposition}[Differential strengthening: an honest negative and a conditional sharpening]\label{prop:radiff}\tagI{}/\tagO{} \hx{RA-C1--RA-C8}
\emph{(a) The naive strengthening fails} (honest negative): $r$ \emph{is}
elementary, $r=(K/\sqrt{z_c})(z^2)^{1/4}=(K/\sqrt{z_c})\exp(\tfrac14\ln(z^2))$, so
it lies in the full elementary-differential closure of the chart field; the
attempt to upgrade the v1.8 valuation certificate to non-membership \emph{there}
breaks, because $d/dz$ shifts valuation by $-1$ and turns the even-valuation
$\rho$ into the odd-valuation $\rho'=z/(1-z^2)$ (parity is not
differential-stable). \emph{(b) The differential-stable sharpening}
\tagO{}\emph{ (conditional)}: under the \emph{unit-log hypothesis} --- the
substrate's only logarithm is $\rho=-\tfrac12\ln(1-z^2)$, a log of the apex
\emph{unit} $1-z^2$ (valuation $0$) --- the apex \emph{monodromy-order} invariant
excludes $r$ from the \emph{unramified} elementary-differential closure
$F_{\mathrm{ed}}$: order-$2$ data ($r$, leading power $z^{1/2}$) is unreachable
from order-$1$ data, while $r^2$ and $\rho'$ are order-$1$ (hence \emph{in}
$F_{\mathrm{ed}}$: indeed $r^2=(K^2/z_c)\,\rho'(1-z^2)$) yet odd-valuation (hence
\emph{outside} the base field $F$). This excludes a \emph{strictly larger} field
than the v1.8 valuation certificate and is differential-stable. It rests on a
finite generator battery plus a leading-exponent argument, not a fully general
closure theorem, and is \tagO{} conditional on the unit-log hypothesis.
\end{proposition}

\begin{remark}[Bounded no-tie re-confirmation of the constructive bridge]\label{rem:ratie}\tagF{}/\tagC{} \hx{RA-D1--RA-D3, RA-A4, RA-G1}
Re-confirming Proposition~\ref{prop:bridgeobstruction} over a finite ledger of
$\sim11$ canonical amplitudes: no canonical amplitude supplies the radius
coefficient $K/\sqrt{z_c}$, since $(K^2/z_c)^2=\tfrac{70}3-10\sqrt5\neq1$ and
differs (exact $\Q(\sqrt5)$, and certified by disjoint intervals) from the square
of every listed amplitude $\{1,K,K^2,\gp,\tau,\gapc,z_c,1/z_c,\sqrt5,2-\gp,
K/\sqrt3\}$. The scale enters only through the odd signed-height generator
$(K^2/z_c)\,z$ --- the D4(i) datum --- never as a canonical scalar.
\end{remark}


\section{Path stretch, rail length, and the conjugate relative phase}\label{sec:pathstretch}

The exploratory note of source (iv) proposes a rail-pulse mechanism for rung
selection. Its mechanism layer is not imported here; this section states and
verifies its \emph{forced kernel} --- three results that use only D1--D3 and
the inherited radius profile --- and records the exact scoping of the rest.

\begin{proposition}[Winding gradient and path stretch]\label{prop:pathstretch}\tagFD{}/\tagI{} \hx{OZ-M1--M3}
From D1, $\rho(z)=-\tfrac12\ln(1-z^2)$, so with $\kappa=\pi/(2\ln\gp)$ [D3],
\[
\frac{d\rho}{dz}=\frac{z}{1-z^2},
\qquad
\frac{d\theta}{dz}=\kappa\,\frac{z}{1-z^2},
\]
and the formation rail $\Gamma(z)=(r\cos\theta,\,r\sin\theta,\,z)$ has local
path-stretch
\[
\frac{ds}{dz}
=\sqrt{1+\Bigl(\frac{dr}{dz}\Bigr)^{2}+r(z)^2\Bigl(\frac{d\theta}{dz}\Bigr)^{2}}
=\begin{cases}
\sqrt{\,1+\dfrac{K^2}{4zz_c}+\dfrac{K^2\kappa^2z^3}{z_c(1-z^2)^2}\,},
& 0<z\le z_c,\\[10pt]
\sqrt{\,1+\dfrac{K^2\kappa^2z^2}{(1-z^2)^2}\,},
& z_c\le z<1,
\end{cases}
\]
with $ds/dz>1$ strictly on $(0,1)$: the helical route is strictly longer than
the vertical displacement, through radial formation and winding below the lens
and through winding alone above it.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
Differentiate D1 and $r(z)$ on each branch and substitute; both closed forms
and the strict excess (all added terms are ratios of positives) are
machine-verified symbolically in $z$.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Quarter-turn rail floor and divergence]\label{prop:railfloor}\tagFD{}/\tagI{} \hx{OZ-M4--M5}
Above the lens, $ds/d\theta=\sqrt{K^2+(dz/d\theta)^2}\ge K$ pointwise, so with
$\Delta\theta=\pi/2$ per rung the rail length between adjacent rungs satisfies
\[
\boxed{\;L_{n\to n+1}\ \ge\ \frac{\pi K}{2}\;}=1.4516928502\ldots\ \tagC{}
\qquad(n\ge2):
\]
every rung interval retains at least a quarter-circle of rail, even as the
vertical increments $z_{n+1}-z_n\to0$. Consequently the cumulative rail length
to the top is infinite while the height stays finite --- a one-line sharpening
of the whitepaper's logarithmic length divergence (W-Prop.\ 15.2c) into a
uniform per-rung floor. Exactly above the lens one also has the identity
$(ds/dz)^2-\bigl(K\kappa z/(1-z^2)\bigr)^2=1$, and the minorant integrates to
$-\tfrac12K\kappa\ln(1-z^2)\to\infty$.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[$\nu$-form of the rung locus]\label{prop:nulocus}\tagFD{} \hx{OZ-M6--M7}
The conjugate pair $\{W,\overline W\}$ exists automatically --- the angle
multiset of a conjugation-closed object is closed under negation (the standing
fact of the relational-charge note, its \S2.2) --- and winds oppositely:
$\theta_\pm(\rho)=\pm\kappa\rho$. Its normalized relative state
\[
\nu(\rho)\ \coloneqq\ \frac{W(\rho)}{\overline{W}(\rho)}\Big/\Bigl|\tfrac{W}{\overline W}\Bigr|
\;=\;e^{2i\theta(\rho)}
\qquad\text{(matrix form: } \mathcal R(\rho)=e^{2J\kappa\rho}\text{)}
\]
is real (scalar) exactly on the rung lattice:
\[
\operatorname{Im}\nu(\rho)=\sin\!\Bigl(\frac{\pi\rho}{\ln\gp}\Bigr)=0
\iff \rho=n\ln\gp,
\qquad
\nu(\rho_n)=(-1)^n ,
\]
and the sign $(-1)^n$ is the Trace--Form-Duality parity bit: $W_n$ is real for
even $n$ (Fibonacci/E--W rungs), imaginary for odd $n$ (Lucas/N--S rungs).
Equivalently, the rungs are exactly the heights at which $W$ is coherent with
the real axis in the sense of the relational note's $\nu$-criterion
($\alpha/\overline\alpha$ a root of unity --- here $\nu(\rho_n)=\pm1$, torsion
of order $\le2$).
\end{proposition}


\begin{proposition}[Site-density trichotomy]\label{prop:trichotomy}\tagFD{}/\tagI{}
\hx{OZ-N1--N2}\ Inverting the rung law gives the continuous floor coordinate ---
and three site densities that must be kept apart. The floor coordinate is
\[
n(z)=\frac{\rho(z)}{\ln\gp}=-\frac{\ln(1-z^2)}{2\ln\gp},
\qquad
\frac{dn}{dz}=\frac{z}{(1-z^2)\ln\gp}\ \xrightarrow[z\to1^-]{}\ \infty
\]
(at the lens, $dn/dz=2\sqrt3/\ln\gp=7.1987042602\ldots$ \tagC{} \hx{OZ-H5}).
All three densities are exact:
\begin{center}\small
\begin{tabular}{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule
measure & behaviour & source\\
\midrule
angular & fixed: $\Delta\theta=\pi/2$, four phase classes per turn & D2$+$D3\\
vertical projection & divergent: $dn/dz\to\infty$ as $z\to1$ & D1\\
rail distance (above lens) & bounded below and convergent: $\Delta s_n\downarrow \pi K/2$ & Prop.~\ref{prop:railfloor}\\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
The third entry sharpens Proposition~\ref{prop:railfloor}:
$dz/d\theta=(1-z^2)/(\kappa z)$ is strictly decreasing to $0$, so the rail
spacing $\Delta s_n=\int\sqrt{K^2+(dz/d\theta)^2}\,d\theta$ is strictly
decreasing with limit \emph{exactly} $\pi K/2$ --- the quarter-turn floor is
attained in the limit. Apparent crowding of rung sites is a projection
artifact of the D1 chart, not an increase in phase classes per revolution.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Reversion norm and the transfer ledger]\label{prop:ledger}\tagFD{} \hx{OZ-N3--N5}
With reversion $\widetilde{aI+bJ}=aI-bJ$ (an anti-automorphism),
\[
Q\widetilde Q=\tau^2 I,
\qquad
I_n \coloneqq Q^n\widetilde{Q^n}=\tau^{2n}I=|W_n|^2 I ,
\]
and the complementary ledger
\[
\boxed{\ F_n \coloneqq 1-I_n = z_n^2\ }
\qquad\text{(D1 at the rungs, in ledger form)},
\]
with per-rung increment, via $1-\tau^2=\tau$,
\[
\Delta F_n=F_{n+1}-F_n=\tau^{2n}(1-\tau^2)=\tau^{2n+1}
=z_{n+1}^2-z_n^2 ,
\qquad
\sum_{n\ge0}\tau^{2n+1}=\frac{\tau}{1-\tau^2}=1 \ \ \text{exactly}.
\]
The odd golden powers $\tau,\tau^3,\tau^5,\ldots$ are precisely the rung
increments of $z^2$, they telescope to the unit ($1-\tau^{2N}=z_N^2$), and the
whole normalized budget is distributed over infinitely many rungs with none
consuming it at finite $n$. The per-step split is $(\tau^2,\tau)$:
transmitted co-intensity $\tau^2$, complementary increment $\tau$. All of this
is exact chart algebra; the source note's reading of $\Delta F_n$ as a
physically \emph{deposited} pulse fraction (its engine-pass model) is an
interpretation and is not imported.
\end{proposition}

\begin{remark}[Scoping: what the rails do and do not close]\label{rem:railscope}
Proposition~\ref{prop:nulocus} removes two of the exploratory note's three
assumptions: the second rail and its counter-rotation are not hypotheses but
the conjugate, forced by conjugation closure. What remains outside the forced
scope is exactly what that note's own \S10(8) concedes: (a) reading the real
crossings of $\nu$ as \emph{recoupling events that cause} rungs is an
interpretation --- with the existing rate $\kappa$ the locus merely coincides
with the ladder already forced by the gate multipliers $|m_n|=\gp^{-2n}$; and
(b) the rate itself is D3 imported, not derived --- for a general rate the
locus is $\rho_n=n\pi/(2\kappa)$, so the construction reproduces the golden
floors only because $\kappa=\pi/(2\ln\gp)$ was put in. Rung quantization (one
$Q$ per floor) therefore remains the open item named in
\S\ref{sec:quarterturn}; the energy condition, the thermodynamic reading, and
the mechanism chain of source (iv) are not imported. The second note (source
(vi)) is handled identically: its forced kernel is
Propositions~\ref{prop:trichotomy}--\ref{prop:ledger}; its engine-pass model,
pulse-budget reading, and biological program remain hypothesis --- correctly
fenced by that note's own claim ceilings and four-layer hierarchy
(geometry $\to$ kinematics $\to$ dynamics $\to$ biology), which this paper
endorses as discipline without importing as content.
\end{remark}

\begin{proposition}[Rail bounds and the gap-rate law]\label{prop:railbounds}\tagFD{}/\tagI{} \hx{QX-E1--E9}
For $n\ge2$ write $t_n=\tau^{2n}$, and on $[\theta_n,\theta_{n+1}]$ set
$x=(dz/d\theta)^2/K^2=t^2/\bigl(K^2\kappa^2(1-t)\bigr)$ with
$t=e^{-2\theta/\kappa}=1-z^2$, so that
$\Delta s_n=K\int_{\theta_n}^{\theta_{n+1}}\sqrt{1+x}\;d\theta$. With
\[
I_1(t)=\frac1{4K\kappa}\Bigl[\ln\frac{1-\tau^2t}{1-t}-\tau t\Bigr]
=\frac1{4K\kappa}\sum_{m\ge2}\frac{1-\tau^{2m}}{m}\,t^m\;>\;0,
\]
\[
I_2(t)=\frac{F(t)-F(\tau^2t)}{16K^3\kappa^3},
\quad
F(t)=\frac1{1-t}+3\ln(1-t)-3(1-t)+\frac{(1-t)^2}2,
\quad
0<I_2(t)\le\frac{1-\tau^8}{64K^7\kappa^3}\,t^4,
\]
the rail spacing of Proposition~\ref{prop:trichotomy} obeys the exact
elementary two-sided bound
\[
\boxed{\ \frac{\pi K}2+I_1(t_n)-I_2(t_n)\ \le\ \Delta s_n\ \le\
\frac{\pi K}2+I_1(t_n)\ }
\]
(lower bound via $\sqrt{1+x}\ge1+\tfrac x2-\tfrac{x^2}8$, valid since
$x\le\gapc^2/(\kappa^2K^4)<8$ on the range, interval-certified
\hx{QX-G1}). Consequences:
\[
\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\Delta s_n-\pi K/2}{\gapc^{\,n}}
=\frac{K}{8\kappa}=\frac{K\ln\gp}{4\pi}=0.0353900591\ldots\ \tagC{},
\qquad
\frac{\Delta s_{n+1}-\pi K/2}{\Delta s_n-\pi K/2}\longrightarrow\gapc,
\]
and since $\gapc^{\,n}=\tau^{4n}=|W_n|^4$,
\[
\Delta s_n-\frac{\pi K}2\ \sim\ \frac{K\ln\gp}{4\pi}\,|W_n|^4:
\]
the excess rail over the quarter-turn floor is priced by the square of the
co-intensity, and the divergent side of the length dichotomy
(Proposition~\ref{prop:sphlength}) is thereby quantified: the rail
diverges at exactly $\pi K/2$ per rung, approached at the gap rate.
Structural note on the exact integral: under the radical,
$(1-z^2)^2+K^2\kappa^2z^2=z^4+(K^2\kappa^2-2)z^2+1$ factors reciprocally
as $(z^2+a)(z^2+1/a)$ with $a+1/a=K^2\kappa^2-2$ (real $a>0$ since
$K\kappa>2$, interval-certified \hx{QX-G2}), placing $\Delta s_n$ in
incomplete-elliptic form --- in contrast with the residual sphere, whose
total length closes in a \emph{complete} elliptic value
(Proposition~\ref{prop:sphlength}); no elementary closed form is claimed
here, and the bounds above carry the elementary content. \hx{QX-E9}
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
From D1$+$D3, $dz/d\theta=(1-z^2)/(\kappa z)$ \hx{QX-E1}. The square-root
bounds are the polynomial identities $(1+x/2)^2-(1+x)=x^2/4$ and
$(1+x)-(1+x/2-x^2/8)^2=x^3(8-x)/64$ \hx{QX-E2}. Substituting
$t=e^{-2\theta/\kappa}$ ($d\theta=-\kappa\,dt/2t$) turns
$\tfrac K2\!\int x\,d\theta$ into
$\tfrac1{4K\kappa}\!\int_{\tau^2t_n}^{t_n}\!\tfrac s{1-s}\,ds$ and
$\tfrac K8\!\int x^2\,d\theta$ into
$\tfrac1{16K^3\kappa^3}\!\int_{\tau^2t_n}^{t_n}\!\tfrac{s^3}{(1-s)^2}\,ds$,
with the stated antiderivatives \hx{QX-E3--E4}; the $m=1$ series term
cancels ($1-\tau^2=\tau$), the $m=2$ term gives the constant via
$1-\tau^4=K^2$ and $1/(8\kappa)=\ln\gp/(4\pi)$ \hx{QX-E5}, and the
majorant uses $(1-s)^2\ge K^4$ on $s\le\gapc$ with
$\int s^3=t^4(1-\tau^8)/4$ \hx{QX-E6}. The ratio law is the series
quotient \hx{QX-E7--E8}. A 50-dps quadrature corroborates the bracket for
$n=2..5$ \hx{QX-G3}; display-only.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Incomplete-elliptic closed form for the rail]\label{thm:railclosed}\tagFD{}/\tagI{} \hx{EX-A1--A4, EX-B1--B8, EX-G3--G5}
Let $a>1$ be the larger root of $a+1/a=K^2\kappa^2-2$ (so
$a\cdot(1/a)=1$; $a$ real with $a>1$ strictly, interval-certified via
$K\kappa>2$, replicating \hx{QX-G2}), and set
\[
\theta(z)=\arctan\!\bigl(z\sqrt a\bigr),\qquad
m=1-\frac1{a^2},\qquad
n_c=1+\frac1a .
\]
Above the lens the rail integrand is
\[
\frac{ds}{dz}=\frac{\sqrt{(z^2+a)(z^2+1/a)}}{1-z^2}
\]
(from $\Delta s_n=\int\sqrt{K^2+(dz/d\theta)^2}\,d\theta$ and
$dz/d\theta=(1-z^2)/(\kappa z)$, with the reciprocal factorization of
Proposition~\ref{prop:railbounds}), and its antiderivative is
\[
\boxed{\ G(z)=-z\sqrt{\frac{z^2+a}{z^2+1/a}}
\;+\;\sqrt a\,E(\theta\,|\,m)
\;-\;a^{-3/2}\,F(\theta\,|\,m)
\;+\;(a+1)\,a^{-3/2}\,\Pi\!\bigl(n_c;\,\theta\,|\,m\bigr)\ },
\]
with $\Delta s_n=G(z_{n+1})-G(z_n)$ at $z_n=\sqrt{1-\tau^{2n}}$
($F,E,\Pi$ the incomplete elliptic integrals of the first, second, and
third kind \tagE{}). Certificate: $dG/dz-{}$integrand${}\equiv0$
exact-symbolically in $(a,z)$ on $a>0$, $0<z<1$ (the forced certificate),
assembled from three kind-pure lemmas through the exact partial-fraction
split of the numerator (the $(1-z^2)^{-1}$ pole, of exact weight $w+2$
with $w=K^2\kappa^2-2$, is what produces the third-kind $\Pi$ term); FTC
applicability on $[z_n,z_{n+1}]\subset(0,1)$ via
$1-n_c\sin^2\theta=(1-z^2)/(1+az^2)>0$. Numeric corroboration: the 50-dps
closed form matches quadrature of $\Delta s_n$ to $<10^{-40}$ on the
\hx{QX-G3} grid $n=2..5$ and sits inside the bracket of
Proposition~\ref{prop:railbounds}. The ``no elementary closed form is
claimed here'' scoping of that proposition is hereby completed on the
positive side: the rail side of the length dichotomy
(Proposition~\ref{prop:sphlength}) is \emph{closed in incomplete-elliptic
terms}.
\end{theorem}

\begin{corollary}[Certified non-elementarity of the rail antiderivative]\label{cor:nonelem}\tagF{}/\tagE{} \hx{EX-C1--C5, EX-G1--G2}
Forced given Liouville's theorem on integration in finite terms
(Rosenlicht 1972; algorithmic form Trager 1984) \tagE{}: the
antiderivative of the above-lens rail integrand is \emph{not} an
elementary function --- the per-rung rail length has no elementary closed
form as a function of its endpoints. All premises of the anchor are
machine-certified: the kernel curve $y^2=(z^2+a)(z^2+1/a)$ is genuinely
elliptic --- $\operatorname{disc}(z^4+wz^2+1)=16(w-2)^2(w+2)^2\neq0$ with
$w=K^2\kappa^2-2>2$, four distinct roots, genus 1 (at $a=1$ it would
collapse to $(z^2+1)^2$ and integrate elementarily); the first-kind
coefficient $c_F=-a^{-3/2}$ is nonzero \emph{unconditionally}
($c_F\,a^{3/2}=-1$ for every $a>0$); $c_E=\sqrt a$ and
$c_\Pi=(a+1)a^{-3/2}$ are nonzero; and the $\Pi$ parameters are
non-degenerate ($m\in(0,1)$ and $n_c\in(1,2)$ interval-certified;
$n_c-1$, $n_c-m$, $1-m$ all exactly nonzero, with the exact interchange
tie $n_c(2-n_c)=m$, so no special-case reduction applies), the $z=\pm1$
pole pair genuine with residues $\mp K\kappa/2\neq0$ and the exact ledger
tie $y(\pm1)^2=(1+a)(1+1/a)=K^2\kappa^2$ (the kernel over the lens poles
is the squared rail-speed constant). The closed-form dichotomy is thus
fully resolved: complete-elliptic on the residual side
(Proposition~\ref{prop:sphlength}); incomplete-elliptic, and provably
non-elementary --- certified rather than conjectured --- on the rail
side, whose elementary content is carried by the bracket and gap-rate law
of Proposition~\ref{prop:railbounds}.
\end{corollary}

\section{Epistemic ledger and findings}\label{sec:epistemics}

\textbf{Forced unconditionally, new in v1.6} \hx{QX-B, QX-C, QX-D}: the

$\operatorname{spec}(A_{\gapc})$, Proposition~\ref{prop:chirat}); the
quarter twist --- transport domain $=$ parity-homogeneous objects,
gauge-rotation invariance, anchor-exchange dichotomy (exchange iff
$m\equiv2$ mod $4$; the generator/$Z^{*}$ pair is the minimal instance and
the K-seed is anchor-fixed) (Theorem~\ref{thm:quartertwist}); the K-family
seed ladder $p_j=x^4+5F_{2j}^2x^2-5F_{2j}^2$ with uniform irreducibility
by norm obstruction, the full ledger, and the gate ties
$C_{2j}=1/|p_j(0)|$, $u_{2j}=1/\M(p_j)$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:kseedladder}).

the exact rail-spacing bounds
$\pi K/2+I_1-I_2\le\Delta s_n\le\pi K/2+I_1$ with the gap-rate law
$\Delta s_n-\pi K/2\sim(K\ln\gp/4\pi)\,|W_n|^4$
(Proposition~\ref{prop:railbounds}); the even-rung seed mirror
$(\sin,\cos,\tan)\alpha_{2j}=(K_j,\tau^{2j},\beta_j)$
(Corollary~\ref{cor:evenmirror}); the transfer of the interpolation
classification to the formation rail, adding the sixth D3 characterization

$\{\Phi_1^{4},\Phi_2^{4}\}$ complete under the full scan bound; all eight
cross-shell ratios real ($\pm\tau^2$, $\pm\gp^2$) with $\nu\equiv1$,
hence full coherence in one class; anchor $(m,n)=(2,4)$ with

schedule (co-intensity at two rungs, amplitude at four); the state-angle

law, and the three-level recurrence; the lens as the $60^\circ$ state; the
path-stretch laws, the quarter-turn rail floor $L\ge\pi K/2$ with its
divergence corollary, the $\nu$-form of the rung locus with parity
$\nu(\rho_n)=(-1)^n$, the site-density trichotomy with
$\Delta s_n\downarrow\pi K/2$, and the transfer ledger $F_n=z_n^2$,
$\Delta F_n=\tau^{2n+1}$, $\sum\tau^{2n+1}=1$ (\S\ref{sec:pathstretch}); the
residual-sphere lift and the colatitude identity $\theta_K=\arccos K$; the
linearization tie $f'(K)=q^2|q|^2$. None of these is a new substrate axiom; all
are compressed representations and corollaries, exactly as the source report
states.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{Declared upstream} \tagD{}/\tagI{}: D1, D2, D3, the orientation
convention, and the inherited radius profile (whitepaper \S11).

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{Not established} (carried verbatim from the source report, endorsed):
biological or DNA correspondence (the direct DNA-pitch test \emph{failed}
quantitatively and is recorded as a negative result; the helicase and
uncouple--recouple language of the v1.1 report functioned as a mechanism-finding
analogy only); physical universality; the residual sphere as fundamental
ontology; derivation of the formation radius from the complex generator;
independent validation of D1--D3 by the representation; \emph{rung
quantization} (why exactly one $Q$ per rung event --- $Q$ explains propagation,
not yet selection); \emph{continuous interpolation as selection} (Theorem~\ref{thm:interp}
classifies the interpolations: a $\Z$-family agreeing at every rung; what the
discrete powers leave undetermined is exactly the principal-branch choice,
which is D3 --- so this item merges into the rate problem); the
rail-pulse mechanism of sources (iv) and (vi) --- recoupling as \emph{cause},
the energy functional $\mathcal E$, the engine-pass/deposition reading of the
transfer ledger, and the biological comparison program --- which their own
epistemic sections correctly leave as hypothesis.
Coordinate-free uniqueness of the factorization, listed open in the source
report, is closed by Corollary~\ref{cor:coordfree}.

\begin{finding}[E-1: scoping slip in the source report's \S11]\label{find:scope}
The report's ``Forced in the whitepaper'' list includes ``quarter-turn charge
increments'' without the ``given D2'' qualifier, inconsistently with its own
``Declared'' list (which correctly names D2) and with its overall conditional
framing. Cosmetic; the report's mathematics nowhere relies on the unqualified
reading. This paper's banner (\S\ref{sec:scope}) restores the qualifier
everywhere.
\end{finding}

The K-family table prints $K_2\approx0.989299$ and $K_3\approx0.998445$; the
exact values $0.9892996329\ldots$ and $0.9984459825\ldots$ round to
$0.989300$ and $0.998446$ at 6 dp --- the printed values are floor-truncations
\hx{OZ-H1}. Display-only; no decision weight. ($K_1$, $K_4$, and $\gapc$ are
correctly rounded.)
\end{finding}

\begin{finding}[E-6: exact-count wording for the first harness]\label{find:ozsplit}
The description ``91 exact checks'' for \texttt{ozy\_softcheck.py}
(\S\ref{sec:scope} and \S\ref{sec:verification}) contradicts that
harness's own manifest table, whose \hx{OZ-H} row lists $5$ numeric

as pass ratios).

\begin{finding}[E-7: the SSS/Lehmer reading of $[\mu_S,2)$ is route-relative]\label{find:sssframe}
v1.9--v2.0 state that the residual gap $[\mu_S,2)$ \emph{is} the
Schur--Siegel--Smyth / Lehmer--pentagon core (Prop.~\ref{prop:oddfloorred}(iv),
\S\ref{blk:odd2}), and infer that closing (ODD-2) requires new number theory
strengthening Smyth. The premise is true only of the \emph{realification}
route: the Adams handle $\psi^m$ discards the ray constraint and keeps only
non-reciprocality, and \emph{within that route} the gap is exactly that core.
It is not a property of (ODD-2). Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd} keeps the ray
constraint --- it transports it through $N_{K/F}$, where odd $m$ re-emerges as
the $\pi$-ray exclusion on $N$ --- and closes the lemma with an elementary
argument, using neither $\mu_S$ nor any strengthening of Smyth. Corrected in
v2.1; no decision elsewhere in the corpus depended on the mis-framing, and the
$\M\le2$ ceiling, the $\pi$-ray exclusion, and the reciprocal floor $\gp^2>2$
are all unaffected. Recorded because the framing, not the mathematics, is what
kept the item open through three versions.
\end{finding}

\begin{finding}[E-8: \texttt{bl\_softcheck.py}'s P3/P4 row is a v2.0 artifact]\label{find:blstale}
The v2.0 finalization harness prints a nine-row blocker ledger in which P3/P4 is
classified \textsc{named-lemma} (ODD-2) and \hx{BL-G4} labels ``the residual
lift $\mu_S\to2$'' as a wall no harness can settle. Both are superseded by
Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd}. As of v2.6 the harness has been brought current: it
marks P3/P4 \textsc{closed}, retires \hx{BL-G4}, and (with the v2.2 cross-shell
discharge) retires \hx{BL-G7}, so its shipped pass line reads \texttt{16/16
exact, 7 walls LABELLED} with \emph{five} active (\hx{BL-G4} and \hx{BL-G7} off
the board). The P3/P4 row is kept in the \S\ref{sec:blockers} table, marked
\textsc{closed}, so that the classification history stays legible; its two
load-bearing forced facts remain true as stated (\hx{BL-E1}: the $=2$ ceiling is
forced; \hx{BL-E2}: Smyth alone gives only $\mu_S<\gp<2$) --- what lapses is the
inference that no further internal route existed.
\end{finding}

\section{The active blockers: status of the open problems}\label{sec:blockers}

This closing section is the definitive, machine-certified statement of exactly
what stands between the corpus and each remaining open question. The corpus's
\emph{forced} content is complete through v1.9; what is recorded here is not new
mathematics but a precise \emph{location of the walls}. Every classification
below, and the single load-bearing forced fact it rests on, is re-certified in
one place by \texttt{bl\_softcheck.py} (groups \hx{BL-A}--\hx{BL-H}), which does
\emph{not} re-derive the corpus but isolates, for each open problem, the one
forced fact that fixes its blocker class and shows each wall-detector
\emph{fail-first} by rejecting a planted false wall. The harness also
\emph{labels} --- without checking, because no harness can settle them --- the
seven walls that lie outside the substrate (\hx{BL-G1}--\hx{BL-G7}). Pass line:
\texttt{BL: EXACT 16/16 checks passed | 7 walls LABELLED}.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.1 motion.} One row has since moved off the board. P3/P4 (the odd
relational floor) is \textsc{closed} by Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd}: \emph{eight}
of the nine rows below remain active, the \textsc{named-lemma} class is empty,
and \hx{BL-G4} is no longer a wall. The closed row is retained in the table
rather than deleted --- the ledger is a record of what blocked what, and a
blocker that fell is part of that record; the harness itself was brought current
in v2.6 (Finding~\ref{find:blstale}).
The eight survivors are untouched by v2.1: nothing in the descent bears on a
declared atom, a restatement wall, or a transcendence tool-gap.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.2 motion.} A second row moves off the board. P1$'$ cross-shell $\nu$
(\S\ref{blk:p1cross}) is \textsc{resolved} by Theorem~\ref{thm:crossnotie}: the
\textsc{unfinished-computation} class is now empty --- its sole occupant executed
and its no-tie proved forced --- \hx{BL-G7} is no longer a wall, and \emph{seven}
of the nine rows remain active. The row is retained and marked, per the same
record-keeping discipline. The seven survivors are untouched by v2.2: the
cross-shell detector bears on no declared atom, no restatement or impossibility
wall, and no external tool-gap.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.7 motions (the completeness-pass harnesses).} Five runnable harnesses
execute the objects \S\ref{blk:required} names, under the completeness pass's
closure grammar: an impossibility is scoped to a \emph{declared} base, and a
certified-independent declared atom \emph{closes} by that horn --- it is not held
open awaiting a principle whose non-arrival is not evidence. \emph{Two rows close.}
\textbf{OP-RADIUS profile / D4} (\S\ref{blk:radprof}) closes as a
certified-independent atom: Theorem~\ref{thm:d4reduction}'s two drop-one witnesses
(orientation $\pm r$; onset $4/5$ versus $z_c$, separated at $z^\ast=5/6$ where
$z^\ast/z_0=25/24>1$) are machine-checked (\texttt{d4\_softcheck}, $18/18$).
\textbf{D2} (\S\ref{blk:d2}) closes as a certified-independent atom: the drop-one
audit that section flagged \emph{unrun} is now run (\texttt{d2\_softcheck}, $37/37$)
--- the pentagon clock $2\pi/5$ survives every forced fact while terrain $\pi$ is
helicity-killed, exhibiting a $\{$D1, D3$'$, keystone$\}$ model that violates D2, so
D2 is independence-certified (battery-relative: a future forced fact naming the
clock would reopen the redundancy horn). \emph{Two rows advance.} \textbf{OP-RATE}'s
continuum horn (\S\ref{blk:rate}) closes \emph{negative} in a declared definability
base $D$ (\texttt{db\_softcheck}, $29/29$): \textsc{DB-1}, a no-section theorem for
the $\log$-torsor of $q$ --- the Cencov mirror of $\lambda=2c$; a positive

\textbf{OP-RADIUS ab initio} (\S\ref{blk:radab}) has step~(a), the generator

pending the apex-closure theorem \textsc{UL-1}, flagged candidate-forced,
\emph{not} proved. Toward the \textbf{free-commutator door}

named target.
The three transcendence rows stay parked on their named external triggers.
\emph{Net:} the internal named objects the corpus could still settle by its own
means drop from three (\S\ref{blk:required}) to \emph{two} --- the $r=1$ Pisot-unit
lemma and the apex-closure theorem --- the D2 drop-one model now produced. No
forced-content theorem is disturbed; each motion is a certificate at its stated
strength (independence-certified, closed-negative-in-$D$, candidate-forced, or
bounded), not an unconditional derivation.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.8 motions (open-tag resolution).} The remaining internal named objects
close at honest strength. \textbf{The $r=1$ floor} (Rem~\ref{rem:evenlocalize}) is
\emph{proved} (Rem~\ref{rem:evenv28}, \texttt{pu\_softcheck} $22/22$): every
totally-real unit $\beta\ne\pm1$ has $\M(\beta)\ge\gp$, so

theorem the v2.7 inventory step left candidate-forced --- is supplied
(\texttt{ul\_closure\_check} $22/22$): every object of the elementary tower over $D5$
has apex monodromy order $1$ (winding lemma $+$ five gate lemmas), while $r$ has
order $2$, so $r\notin F_{\mathrm{ed}}(D5)$; but the argument runs inside a
\emph{declared} ambient (log-exp transseries / a $\sigma$-germ ring), so the row
closes by \textsc{impossibility} \emph{relative to that ambient}, the OP-RATE
closed-negative-in-$D$ grammar, the ambient carried as the one named hinge --- it is
\emph{not} unconditional and reverts to candidate-forced if the ambient is rejected
as the reading of the unramified elementary-differential closure.
\textbf{The free-commutator door} (Finding~\ref{find:gapdichotomy}) is \emph{full at
every even $n$}: the catalog $\otimes/\oplus$-algebra $A_n=M_n(\Q)$ by an $n$-uniform
matrix-unit induction whose eight window-local $\Q(\sqrt5)$ certificates are
$n$-independent (\texttt{fc\_unbounded\_check} $24/24$), with the first trace-zero
Salem commutator at $n=6$. This \emph{discharges} \S\ref{blk:required}'s
full-matrix-algebra requirement; and in v2.9 the dichotomy's
\emph{inadmissible-traffic} half --- whether every inadmissible object walks through
the door --- is \emph{resolved} as the Door-Traffic Dichotomy
(Theorem~\ref{thm:doortraffic}): exact passage iff trace-balanced (\tagF{} outright),
escorted-universal otherwise (\tagF{} given $A_n=M_n(\Q)$, with the classical
\tagE{} Shoda / Albert--Muckenhoupt realization load-bearing), Lehmer permanently
escort-only, forced onset $n=6$ --- and it carries \emph{no} claim on Lehmer's
problem (measures accumulating at $1$).
\textbf{Within-shell} (Rem~\ref{rem:p1scan}): the conjectured $p=s(x^k)$ taxonomy is
\emph{refuted} by four exact unit-twist witnesses (irreducible, non-cyclotomic, with
a \emph{proper} torsion tie $p\mid g(x^n)$; the order-$3$ pair are the golden cubes
$\gp^{-3},-\gp^3$), and the mechanism lemmas close degree-free: \textbf{L1}
(root-multiset $\zeta_k$-closure $\iff$ $p=s(x^k)$) and \textbf{L2}
($g=\mathrm{minpoly}(\alpha^k)$ gives $p\mid g(x^k)$, and $2\le t=d/\deg g\le k$ since
$\alpha$ satisfies $X^k-\alpha^k$ over $\Q(\alpha^k)$), partitioning every
within-shell tie into cyclotomic / radical / saturated-tower ($t=k$) / unit-twist
($2\le t<k$) (\texttt{ws\_structure\_check} $23/23$). But the \emph{exhaustiveness} of
this taxonomy over the unbounded-degree \emph{family} is \emph{not} proved and
\emph{stays} \tagC{}-open, exactly as the corpus records: L4's degree cap
($[E:\Q]=32$, $E=\Q(i,\sqrt2,\sqrt3,5^{1/4})$, so every catalog-word factor has degree
$\le32$) is true but bounds only the \emph{catalog-word} population, not the general
integer-polynomial family the scan ranges over. \emph{Net} (the honest count,
\S\ref{blk:required}): the two internal named objects resolve --- the $r=1$ lemma
unconditionally, UL-1 conditionally on the declared ambient; the door's
full-matrix requirement discharges and (v2.9) its traffic is resolved as a
dichotomy, conditional on fullness ($A_n=M_n(\Q)$) and the classical \tagE{}
trace-zero realization; within-shell gains a refutation and a mechanism theorem
while its exhaustiveness stays \tagC{}-open (a wider degree-$10$ scan corroborates,
never settles). This is \emph{not} ``everything closed'' --- within-shell
exhaustiveness and the three transcendence rows remain open. No forced-content
theorem is disturbed.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{The six blocker classes.} Each open problem is filed under exactly one
(for OP-RATE, a dual) of the following.
\begin{description}[font=\normalfont\scshape,leftmargin=2.2em,style=nextline,itemsep=1pt,topsep=3pt]
\item[impossibility] a forced no-go: the resolution cannot come from the stated
axioms. A hard wall, always scoped \emph{relative} to D1--D3 (equivalently,
relative to the discrete substrate), never an absolute claim.
\item[declared-atom] an irreducible declaration the substrate does not force;
resolvable only by adding a new principle.
\item[restatement-wall] every proposed reduction is logically equivalent to the
axiom; the residual content is irreducible.
\item[named-lemma] reduced to a single precisely-stated open lemma requiring new
(internal) mathematics. \emph{As of v2.1 this class is empty}: its sole
occupant, ODD-2, is proved (Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd}). The class is retained
because it is the one class whose occupancy the corpus could always have
discharged by its own means --- and, in the event, did.
\item[external-tool-gap] blocked on a theorem that does not exist (no tool
reaches the regime) or a named conjecture.
\item[unfinished-computation] tractable in principle, not yet executed.
\emph{As of v2.2 this class is empty}: its sole occupant, the P1$'$ cross-shell
$\nu$-criterion, is executed and its no-tie proved forced
(Theorem~\ref{thm:crossnotie}).
\end{description}
Throughout, an \textsc{impossibility} verdict asserts that the resolution cannot
be a consequence of D1--D3 (or of the discrete substrate); it is a statement
\emph{about the axiom system}, never an absolute impossibility in mathematics at
large.

\subsection{OP-RATE: deriving the winding rate}\label{blk:rate}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{closed as a classification} (v1.8), open only as a
continuum derivation. \textbf{Blocker:} dual --- \textsc{impossibility}
(discrete) $+$ \textsc{declared-atom} (D3). \textbf{Forced fact:} the discrete
rung state is $k$-invariant --- every branch $\kappa_k=(4k+1)\kappa$ yields the
identical rung values $i^{\,n}$ (\hx{BL-A1}; origin \texttt{cl}/\texttt{dx},
Lemma~\ref{lem:kinvext}, Theorem~\ref{thm:r6nogo}) --- so \emph{every} functional
of the discrete state is branch-blind and cannot single out $k=0$. This is an
\textsc{impossibility} \emph{relative to the discrete substrate}: no
discrete-level principle derives the rate (Corollary~\ref{cor:opratelass}). The
residual is then the continuum rate D3, a \textsc{declared-atom}.
\textbf{Missing object:} a principle forcing D3 from something more primitive
than a declaration; none exists in the corpus. A continuum-referencing
derivation is not excluded (it would live outside the discrete substrate), so
the classification is closed while the derivation stays open.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.7 motion --- closed-negative in a declared base.} The
``continuum-referencing derivation'' is given a \emph{declared} base and closed
\emph{negative} within it (\texttt{db\_softcheck}, $29/29$). In the base $D$ of
functionals of the branch-blind skeleton that factor through the marking-relabel
$T$, the markings $\{\log_k q\}$ form a free transitive $\Z$-set and every
$D$-functional is constant on the orbit; hence \textsc{DB-1}: no $D$-definable
section of the $\log$-torsor of $q$ exists, and $\kappa$ is declared-up-to-$\Z$
inside $D$ --- the exact Cencov mirror of $c$ in $\lambda=2c$
(Corollary~\ref{cor:opratelass}). The escape clause is named, not excluded: a
positive derivation must introduce a non-$T$-invariant atom and clear the seven-way
restatement table (Remark~\ref{rem:sevenway}).

\subsection{OP-RADIUS ab initio}\label{blk:radab}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{open as an ab-initio derivation}; whitepaper
\tagO{}~1 is \emph{not} closed. \textbf{Blocker:} \textsc{impossibility} (apex
parity). \textbf{Forced fact:} at the apex the inherited radius-squared has odd
valuation, $v_z(r^2)=1$, whereas every chart quantity has valuation in the value
group $2\Z$ (\hx{BL-B1}/\hx{BL-B2}; origin \texttt{rd}/\texttt{rx},
Theorem~\ref{thm:apexram}, Theorem~\ref{thm:paritynogo}); a planted ``$r^2$ is
even'' is rejected fail-first (\hx{BL-B3}). \textbf{Missing object:} the odd
generator --- the signed square root $\sqrt z$ --- is provably outside the
chart's differential closure and must be \emph{adjoined}; deriving $r$ from
D1--D3 alone is therefore impossible \emph{relative to those axioms}.
\textbf{Honest scope:} this impossibility is scoped to D1--D3; $r$ \emph{itself}
is an elementary function ($r=(K/\sqrt{z_c})\,(z^2)^{1/4}$,
Proposition~\ref{prop:radiff}). The wall is not that $r$ is exotic, but that the
substrate's even-parity chart cannot reach it.
\textbf{Scope caveat (v2.3).} The no-go is proved at the \emph{chart-rational}
level ($r^2\notin\Q(\sqrt5)(z^2)$; odd apex valuation). W-\S23.1 asks the
\emph{gate-family / elementary-differential} question one level up, and there $r$
\emph{is} elementary (Proposition~\ref{prop:radiff}), so parity --- which
$\tfrac{d}{dz}$ shifts and so does not preserve --- is not the deciding invariant
at that level. The statement that answers the question actually asked lives at the
\emph{unramified} elementary-differential closure, where $r$'s apex monodromy
order $2$ (a branch point) excludes it --- but that is \emph{conditional on the
unit-log hypothesis} (Proposition~\ref{prop:radiff}), and closing W\tagO{}~1 by
\textsc{impossibility} at that strength needs a localized structure theorem (a
Rosenlicht/Risch localization carrying a monodromy-order invariant), an
\emph{internal} named missing object. Parity settles the chart level; the
gate-family level stays conditional.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.7 motion --- step (a) discharged; row still open.} The generator
inventory $D5$ --- step (a) of the required apex-localized structure theorem --- is
discharged (\texttt{ul\_softcheck}, $25/25$). Over the chart field the winding lemma
$n_0(u)=\mathrm{res}_0(u'/u)=v_z(u)$ certifies every $D5$-built object (field
operations, $d/dz$, $\exp$, $\log$ of an apex \emph{unit}, unramified algebraic
extensions) at apex monodromy order~$1$, whereas $r$ has order~$2$ ($v_z(r^2)=1$),
and the sole order-raising composite $\exp(c\log z)$ is excluded exactly by the
unit-log gate ($\log z$ is inadmissible, $n_0(z)=1\ne0$). Given the inventory,
$r\notin F_{\mathrm{ed}}(D5)$ and the row would close by \textsc{impossibility}
relative to $D1$--$D3+D5$ --- \emph{but} the general apex-closure theorem
\textsc{UL-1} (structural induction over the whole $D5$ tower) is flagged
\texttt{[candidate-forced, pending second-lane audit]}, \emph{not} proved. The row
stays open, advanced from ``named missing object'' to ``one candidate-forced theorem
away''.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.8 motion --- closed-negative relative to a declared ambient.} The
candidate-forced theorem \textsc{UL-1} is now supplied (\texttt{ul\_closure\_check},
$22/22$). Reading the tower inside a \emph{declared} ambient --- grid-based log-exp
transseries, or equivalently a $\sigma$-spreadable germ ring at the apex --- the
winding lemma $n_0(u)=\mathrm{res}_0(u'/u)$ extends to an integer grading, and the
five gate operations (field ops, $d/dz$, $\exp$, $\log$ of an apex unit, unramified
algebraic extension) each preserve apex monodromy order $1$; the sole order-raising
composite $\exp(c\log z)$ is barred by the unit-log gate ($n_0(z)=1\ne0$), and the
unramified case runs through Newton--Puiseux with the admissible slopes exactly the
integers ($Y^2=z^2(1+z)$ admitted at slope $1$, $Y^2=z^3(1+z)$ refused at slope
$3/2$). Since $r$ has order $2$ ($v_z(r^2)=1$, forcing $\sigma r=-r$), $r\notin
F_{\mathrm{ed}}(D5)$: the row closes by \textsc{impossibility} \emph{relative to
$D1$--$D3+D5$ and the declared ambient}, in the exact grammar of OP-RATE's
closed-negative-in-$D$. The ambient is the \emph{one} hinge --- its identification
with the ``unramified elementary-differential closure'' is a declared modeling
reading, not a theorem, so the closure is \emph{conditional}: if the reading is
rejected, UL-1 reverts to candidate-forced. The monodromy order is the invariant
Rem~\ref{rem:evenlocalize}'s sibling row (\S\ref{blk:required}) named, and step (a)'s
winding lemma is its computable certificate in the meromorphic regime.

\subsection{OP-RADIUS profile, given the bridge}\label{blk:radprof}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{reduced} to the minimal declared atom D4 (v1.9);
given D4 and the inherited chart-ties the profile $r(z)=K\sqrt{z/z_c}$ (capped) is
uniquely forced (Theorem~\ref{thm:d4reduction}). \textbf{Blocker:}
\textsc{declared-atom} (D4). \textbf{Forced fact:} D4 has two irreducible halves,
each a free datum the substrate does not fix --- the \emph{orientation} of the
apex double cover is a free $\Z/2$ sign ($+r$ and $-r$ both square to $r^2$ yet
differ, \hx{BL-C1}), and the \emph{cap onset} is a free choice ($z_c$ versus,
e.g., $4/5$ give distinct capped profiles, \hx{BL-C2}); origin \texttt{ra}. Each
half is \emph{necessary} (Theorem~\ref{thm:d4reduction}). \textbf{Missing
object:} a formation-dynamics principle that forces the cap onset at $z_c$, plus
an orientation of the apex double cover (\hx{BL-G5}). Until such a principle is
added, D4 is declared, not derived; and ``D4 is \emph{the} minimal
axiomatization'' is itself only a candidate (the kink-slope-plus-sign atom is
equivalent, Remark~\ref{rem:d4candidate}).

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.7 motion --- closes under the grammar.} The completeness pass reads the
``missing principle'' as an \emph{undeclared base}. Theorem~\ref{thm:d4reduction}
already exhibits a drop-one witness for each half of D4 --- orientation ($+r$ and
$-r$ chart-consistent yet $2K\ne0$) and onset ($z_0=4/5$ a distinct chart-consistent
capped profile, on its cap at $z^\ast=5/6$ since $z^\ast/z_0=25/24>1$, while the
$z_c$ horn is below cap there, $(r/K)^4=25/27$) --- which is exactly an
\emph{independence certificate}. \texttt{d4\_softcheck} ($18/18$) machine-checks both
witnesses, so under the closure grammar a certified-independent declared atom closes
by horn~(c): the row \emph{closes}. D4 stays declared, but ``derive D4'' is answered,
negatively, relative to $\{$D1, D3, keystone$\}$, and the kink-slope/onset bijection
(Remark~\ref{rem:d4candidate}) makes ``the minimal axiomatization'' a representative
choice, not a residual open problem.

\subsection{W\texorpdfstring{$[\text{open}]$}{[open]}2 / D2: the chi-selection theorem}\label{blk:d2}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{advanced and substrate-grounded, still
PARTIAL as a derivation} (v1.9); D2's content is relocated to a
substrate-grounded, g1-disjoint atom, an advance, not a closure
(Remark~\ref{rem:d2verdict}). \textbf{Blocker:} \textsc{restatement-wall}.
\textbf{Forced fact:} the K-seed rotation pair $\pm i\beta$ sits \emph{off} the
unit circle ($|i\beta|^2=\gp^4-1>1$, \hx{BL-D1}), so it is genuinely disjoint
from the on-circle-image obstruction g1; but naming that K-seed clock is
logically \emph{equivalent} to the quarter-turn selection over the competing
quanta $\{$terrain $\pi:\Z/2$, pentagon $2\pi/5:\Z/5$, quarter $\pi/2:\Z/4\}$
(\hx{BL-D2}; origin \texttt{qt} / QT-C3/C5, Proposition~\ref{prop:d2reloc}).
Under the relocated atom $\chi=\pm\pi/2$ becomes the \emph{forced} argument of a
forced object and the $\Z/4\Z$ completion a forced output --- but the atom is not
thinner than D2. \textbf{Missing object:} a principle forcing the clock-choice
from something more primitive than the quarter-turn selection; none exists in the
corpus (\hx{BL-G6}). Every candidate reduction is logically equivalent to D2, so
its residual content is irreducible.
\textbf{Audit not run (v2.3).} The drop-one-witness test is applied to the
keystone's four constraints (W-\S3) and to the $\lambda=2c$ atoms, but not to
D1--D4 themselves. Theorem~\ref{thm:factorization}'s unconditional $J=\pm UA$
already forces the quarter-turn's \emph{existence} and the angle lattice
$(\pi/2)\Z$; whether it also forces D2's residual \emph{selection} given D3's rate
--- i.e.\ whether a model of $\{$D1, D3, keystone algebra$\}$ can violate D2 ---
is a distinct question the restatement-wall verdict does not answer. The wall says
every proposed \emph{reduction} restates D2; an \emph{independence} audit (a
drop-one model) has not been run, and if D2 proved redundant the row would
\emph{retire} rather than close. Flagged as an open audit, not a resolution.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{v2.7 motion --- closes under the grammar.} The independence audit above is
now \emph{run} (\texttt{d2\_softcheck}, $37/37$). Freeing the clock to $\chi$
unspecified (D3$'$), the sole real discriminant is helicity
(W-Thm.~11.1): $\chi=\pi$ (terrain) is
killed because $e^{i\pi}=-1$ is real, while $\chi=2\pi/5$ (pentagon) survives every
forced fact --- Proposition~\ref{prop:d2reloc}'s on-circle exclusion needs the
unsatisfiable Reading~A (Remark~\ref{rem:chidoctrine}, on the on-circle image
Proposition~\ref{prop:oncircleimage}), and the K-seed argument needs
D2's own rotation-axis selection. The surviving pentagon clock is a model of
$\{$D1, D3$'$, keystone$\}$ that violates D2 (its angle lattice
$\langle1/5\rangle$ cannot realise the $\Z/4\Z$ of the K-seed), so D2 is a
\emph{genuine} atom, \emph{independence-certified}: the row closes by horn~(c)
rather than retiring. Independence is battery-relative --- a future forced fact
naming the clock would reopen the redundancy horn --- and D2 stays declared;
``derive D2'' is answered, negatively, relative to the battery.

\subsection{P3/P4: the odd relational floor equals two --- \textsc{closed}}\label{blk:odd2}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{proved} (v2.1). $\mu_{\mathrm{rel}}(\text{odd
}m)=2$ for every odd $m$, unconditionally (Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd}); P4
inherits it exactly (Remark~\ref{rem:p4transfer}). \textbf{Blocker:}
\textsc{none}. This row is retained, and the v2.0 text below preserved in
substance, because the way it was mis-classified is the useful part of the
record (Finding~\ref{find:sssframe}).

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{What was true, and stays true.} The $=2$ \emph{ceiling} is forced ---
$\M(x^m-2)=2$ for odd $m$ ($x^m-2$ is Eisenstein at $2$, all roots at modulus
$2^{1/m}>1$, \hx{BL-E1}); Smyth's theorem alone gives only $\mu_S<\gp<2$ (the
plastic number $\mu_S=1.3247$, \hx{BL-E2}); the real-reciprocal sub-case is
forced $\ge\gp^2>2$ (\hx{BL-E3}); and the naive ``non-reciprocal $+$ Smyth
forces $\ge2$'' idea is false. \textbf{What was wrong.} v2.0 recorded the
missing object as ``a Mahler-measure lower bound of $2$ in that
charge-constrained regime --- new number theory strengthening Smyth'', and
labelled the residual lift $\mu_S\to2$ an unsettleable wall (\hx{BL-G4}). Both
inferences ran through the realification route, which discards the ray
constraint; they are properties of that route, not of the lemma
(Finding~\ref{find:sssframe}). \textbf{What supplied it.} Not a strengthening of
Smyth --- a change of route. The relative norm $N=N_{K/F}(\alpha)$,
$F=\Q(\alpha^m)$, carries the ray constraint forward: $N$ is totally positive
because $\pi\notin(2\pi/m)\Z$ for odd $m$, and $N\neq1$ because $\alpha$ is not
a root of unity. A totally positive algebraic integer $N\neq1$ has $\M(N)\ge2$
by an elementary argument (Lemma~\ref{lem:tp2}: $|g(1)|\ge1$ plus convexity of
$\log(1+e^t)$), and $e\mid d'$ makes the descent exponent $d'/e$ a positive
integer, so $\M(\alpha)\ge\M(N)^{d'/e}\ge2$. Sharp at $x^m-2$. Certified over a
forced population of $565$ objects, $0$ failures, including the $20$ objects with
$1<k<m$ that no degree split reaches \hx{ND-N1--ND-N5}. \textbf{Cost to the
corpus:} none --- no declared atom, no external theorem, no new axiom.

\subsection{Transcendence of \texorpdfstring{$L_{\mathrm{res}}$}{L\_res}}\label{blk:lrestrans}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{external-open}, with a named tool-gap; not
resolved here. \textbf{Blocker:} \textsc{external-tool-gap}. \textbf{Forced
fact:} via the M\"obius correspondence $m=\kappa^2/(1+\kappa^2)$, the elliptic
modulus $m$ is transcendental if and only if $\kappa$ is transcendental
(\hx{BL-F1}; origin \texttt{pf}, Remark~\ref{rem:periodmap}); and $\kappa$ is
transcendental (Theorem~\ref{thm:ratetrans}), so the modulus is transcendental.
\textbf{Missing object:} a transcendence theorem for elliptic periods at a
\emph{transcendental} modulus. No such theorem exists --- Schneider's and
Chudnovsky's results require an \emph{algebraic} modulus, and ours is provably
transcendental. This is labelled \hx{BL-G1}, not checked: the paper names the
tool-gap and does not claim the transcendence.

\subsection{Algebraic independence of \texorpdfstring{$\pi$}{pi} and \texorpdfstring{$\ln\gp$}{ln phi}}\label{blk:pilnphi}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{conditional on Schanuel}; not resolved here.
\textbf{Blocker:} \textsc{external-tool-gap} (a named conjecture).
\textbf{Forced fact:} the premise $\{\ln\gp,\,i\pi\}$ is $\Q$-linearly
independent is forced, and Baker's theorem delivers the $\overline\Q$-linear
independence of $\{1,\ln2,\ln3,\ln\gp\}$ (\hx{BL-F3}; origin \texttt{pf},
Remark~\ref{rem:pffrontier}) --- but linear independence is all Baker reaches.
\textbf{Missing object:} Schanuel's conjecture, which would upgrade the forced
linear independence to algebraic independence of $\pi$ and $\ln\gp$. Labelled
\hx{BL-G2}; the conclusion is Schanuel-conditional and is \emph{not} asserted.

\subsection{Algebraic independence of \texorpdfstring{$\kappa$}{kappa} and \texorpdfstring{$L_{\mathrm{res}}$}{L\_res}}\label{blk:kappalres}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{external-open}, beyond Schanuel; not resolved
here. \textbf{Blocker:} \textsc{external-tool-gap} (period-relation theory).
\textbf{Forced fact:} $L_{\mathrm{res}}$ is an elliptic value (a period) lying
\emph{outside} the exponential/logarithm framework in which Schanuel operates
(origin \texttt{pf}, Remark~\ref{rem:periodmap}); the certified PSLQ search finds
no integer relation among the corpus bases at $230$ dps, height $\le10^{12}$
(\tagC{} corroboration only). \textbf{Missing object:} Kontsevich--Zagier
period-relation theory (conjectural) --- the framework that would decide
relations among periods. Labelled \hx{BL-G3}; the independence is neither proved
nor claimed, and the PSLQ non-relation corroborates but never proves it.

\subsection{P1\texorpdfstring{$'$}{'} cross-shell nu-criterion --- resolved (v2.2)}\label{blk:p1cross}
\textbf{Status} (register): \emph{resolved} (v2.2). Within-shell classification
delivered (v1.8); the cross-shell $\nu$-criterion half is now \emph{proved} a
no-tie (Theorem~\ref{thm:crossnotie}). \textbf{Former blocker:}
\textsc{unfinished-computation} --- a cross-shell coherence detector, tractable in
principle and simply not yet executed. \textbf{Resolution:} the detector was
built, validated fail-first against Theorem~\ref{thm:reltype} (it reproduces the
generator's $\nu\equiv1$ and discriminates the Salem/Pisot non-coherent objects),
and run; and the equivalence it computes was \emph{proved forced} --- for a
multi-shell object, cross-shell coherence $\iff$ charge-admissibility, by
conjugation-closure ($\theta_\alpha\pm\theta_\beta\in\pi\Q\Rightarrow
\theta_\alpha\in\pi\Q$). The within-shell $\mathrm{OTHER}=0$ verdict is thereby
the complete cross-shell statement: no object carries a cross-shell mechanism
absent from its within-shell class. Corroborated over the same $855$-object
forced window with $0$ exceptions (\texttt{xs\_softcheck.py}, \hx{XS-C2}).
\hx{BL-G7} is discharged; this was the sole \textsc{unfinished-computation}
\emph{blocker row}, and that class is now \emph{empty}. This is distinct from the
\emph{general} within-shell taxonomy over unbounded degree, which is a \tagC{}
corroboration (Remark~\ref{rem:p1scan}), \emph{never} a \S\ref{sec:blockers} row,
and stays \tagC{}: a wider scan corroborates but does not \emph{settle} an
infinite family. Extending Remark~\ref{rem:p1scan}'s window past its
degree-$6$ cap (e.g.\ to Lehmer's degree $10$) is thus a runnable corroboration,
not a blocker whose non-execution the ledger tracks.

\subsection{What is required, theoretically}\label{blk:required}
The v2.3--v2.5 reading lets the ledger state, for each remaining open item, the
\emph{precise theoretical object} whose production would close it. This is not new
mathematics; it is the exact shape of what each closure needs, sorted by whether
the object is \emph{internal} (a named lemma or model the corpus could in principle
produce), \emph{scoped-external} (a specific piece of existing mathematics is
missing but applicable), or \emph{conjecture-external} (blocked on a named open
conjecture).

\begin{center}\footnotesize\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{3.5cm}p{1.9cm}p{8.3cm}@{}}
\toprule
open item & kind & the theoretical object required\\
\midrule
even floor, fully internal (Rem~\ref{rem:evenlocalize}) & internal --- named lemma & an elementary $\M\ge\gp$ for totally-real Pisot \emph{units} (the $r=1$ residual, after the $N^2$ peel reduces every $r\ge2$ case to Lemma~\ref{lem:tp2}); it closes the $(\sqrt2,\gp)$ window by discriminant/trace integrality. With it the corpus carries \emph{no} external floor. \emph{v2.8: \textsc{proved} (Rem~\ref{rem:evenv28}); import removed.}\\[2pt]
OP-RADIUS ab initio, W\tagO{}1 (\S\ref{blk:radab}) & internal --- structure theorem & a Rosenlicht/Risch integration-in-finite-terms structure theorem \emph{localized at the apex} with a monodromy-order invariant, placing $r$ (order $2$) outside the unramified elementary-differential closure under the unit-log hypothesis. \emph{v2.8: \textsc{supplied} relative to a declared ambient (\S\ref{blk:radab} motion); UL-1 closes conditionally.}\\[2pt]
W\tagO{}2 / D2 (\S\ref{blk:d2}) & internal --- drop-one model & a model of D1, D3 and the keystone algebra that \emph{violates} D2. If none exists, D2 is redundant given Theorem~\ref{thm:factorization}$+$D3 and the row \emph{retires}; if one exists, D2 is a genuine atom. \emph{Run in v2.7} (\texttt{d2\_softcheck}): the pentagon clock supplies the model, so D2 is a genuine atom (independence-certified).\\[2pt]
OP-RATE derivation (\S\ref{blk:rate}) & scoped-external & a continuum-referencing selection principle --- a no-section theorem for the $\log$-torsor of $q$ in a fixed definability framework --- forcing the $k=0$ marking from data the $k$-invariant discrete state cannot see.\\[2pt]
OP-RADIUS profile / D4 (\S\ref{blk:radprof}) & scoped-external --- dynamics & a formation-dynamics principle fixing the cap onset at $z_c$ and an orientation of the apex double cover: the two free halves of D4.\\[2pt]
no-Salem dichotomy, inadmissible half (Finding~\ref{find:gapdichotomy}) & scoped-external --- commutator & the free-commutator analysis (W-\S5.2): does the associative algebra generated by the $\otimes/\oplus$-images of the catalog contain a full matrix algebra? The admissible half is Corollary~\ref{cor:gapuniv}. \emph{v2.8: \textsc{discharged} --- $A_n=M_n(\Q)$ at every even $n$ (\texttt{fc\_unbounded\_check}). v2.9: the inadmissible-\emph{traffic} half is \textsc{resolved} as the Door-Traffic Dichotomy (Thm~\ref{thm:doortraffic}) --- exact iff trace-balanced (\tagF{}), escorted-universal otherwise (\tagF{} given $A_n$ $+$ \tagE{} realization); no claim on Lehmer's problem.}\\[2pt]
$L_{\mathrm{res}}$ transcendence (\S\ref{blk:lrestrans}) & conjecture-external & a transcendence theorem for an elliptic value at a \emph{transcendental} modulus --- beyond Schneider/Chudnovsky, which need an algebraic modulus or CM.\\[2pt]
$(\pi,\ln\gp)$ independence (\S\ref{blk:pilnphi}) & conjecture-external & Schanuel's conjecture (the premise, $\{\ln\gp,i\pi\}$ $\Q$-linearly independent, is already forced).\\[2pt]
$(\kappa,L_{\mathrm{res}})$ independence (\S\ref{blk:kappalres}) & conjecture-external & Kontsevich--Zagier period-relation theory (an elliptic period, beyond Schanuel).\\

\end{tabular}
\end{center}
\noindent The honest count of what the corpus \emph{could} still settle by its own
means was \emph{three named objects}: the $r=1$ Pisot-unit lemma
(Rem~\ref{rem:evenlocalize}), the apex-localized structure theorem
(\S\ref{blk:radab}), and the D2 drop-one model (\S\ref{blk:d2}). \emph{The D2 model
is produced in v2.7} (\texttt{d2\_softcheck}), leaving \emph{two}; and OP-RADIUS ab
initio's inventory step is discharged (\texttt{ul\_softcheck}), reducing its
structure theorem to one candidate-forced induction. The two scoped-external items
are addressed at the harness level in v2.7 --- OP-RATE's no-section is certified in a
declared base (\texttt{db\_softcheck}) and OP-RADIUS profile / D4 closes as a
certified-independent atom (\texttt{d4\_softcheck}), re-reading its ``dynamics
principle'' as an undeclared base --- while three wait on named conjectures that do
not yet exist. Nothing here is an unattempted derivation --- each is a
precisely-shaped object. \emph{v2.8:} both remaining internal named objects now
resolve --- the $r=1$ Pisot-unit lemma is \emph{proved} (Rem~\ref{rem:evenv28}), and
the apex-localized structure theorem is \emph{supplied} (\S\ref{blk:radab})
\emph{relative to a declared ambient}, closing UL-1 conditionally in the OP-RATE
grammar. The scoped-external commutator item is \emph{discharged} at every even $n$
(\texttt{fc\_unbounded\_check}: $A_n=M_n(\Q)$), and \emph{in v2.9 its
inadmissible-traffic half is resolved} as the Door-Traffic Dichotomy
(Theorem~\ref{thm:doortraffic}), conditional on that fullness and the classical
\tagE{} trace-zero realization; the within-shell $p=s(x^k)$ conjecture is
\emph{refuted} with its mechanism proved degree-free (\texttt{ws\_structure\_check};
the occupancy scan extended cold through degree $10$ in v2.9,
\texttt{open\_tags\_resolution\_check}). So the count of \emph{named objects the
corpus must still produce} drops to \emph{zero} --- carrying exactly two honest
caveats: UL-1's closure rests on the declared ambient, and the within-shell taxonomy
\emph{exhaustiveness} stays \tagC{}-open (a mechanism partition, not a
proven-occupied classification at unbounded degree). The three conjecture-external
rows are unchanged.

\smallskip\noindent
\textbf{Closing.} The corpus's forced content is complete through v1.9; v2.1 adds
the odd relational floor, proved $=2$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:odd2nd}), and v2.2 adds
the cross-shell $\nu$-criterion no-tie (Theorem~\ref{thm:crossnotie}). What
remains falls into \emph{four} of the six blocker classes above --- both the
\textsc{named-lemma} class (discharged from inside in v2.1, by an elementary
argument requiring no external theorem and no new axiom) and the
\textsc{unfinished-computation} class (executed, and its output proved forced, in
v2.2) are now \emph{empty}. \emph{Three are hard walls internal to the axiom
system} --- the two impossibilities (OP-RATE at discrete resolution and OP-RADIUS
ab initio) and the restatement wall (D2); \emph{two are single declared atoms}
(D4's cap-onset and orientation); and \emph{three are external tool-gaps} (the
transcendence and algebraic-independence questions, blocked respectively on a
nonexistent transcendental-modulus theorem, on Schanuel's conjecture, and on
Kontsevich--Zagier period theory). \emph{Seven rows, not eight.} No remaining \S\ref{sec:blockers}
row is a mere oversight or an unattempted derivation. The honest scope of that
claim is \emph{by the routes the corpus had reached for}, and v2.3 shows the audit
is never finished: outside the seven-row ledger, the rung-1 seed's floor hit was a
settleable identity ($p_1=q_2$, Remark~\ref{rem:mahlerfloor}) carried three
versions as a coincidence-candidate, and the \emph{even} relational floor
(Theorem~\ref{thm:evenfloor}) was reachable by the odd descent with Schinzel in
place of TP-2 --- both settleable, both unsettled until named. That is
Finding~\ref{find:sssframe}'s lesson one level up: a completeness pass over the
whitepaper's tags against these rows is itself an open audit. Each of the seven
survivors is a precisely located wall,
and for each this paper states the load-bearing forced fact and the exact missing
object rather than claiming a resolution --- and, per the honesty gate, every
\textsc{impossibility} is scoped relative to D1--D3 and no external-open item is
asserted to be resolved. One caution the ODD-2 and cross-shell closures earn
their own ledger: a blocker classification is a statement about the routes in
hand --- \S\ref{blk:odd2} sat at \textsc{named-lemma} for three versions on the
strength of a framing (Finding~\ref{find:sssframe}) rather than a theorem, and
\S\ref{blk:p1cross} sat at \textsc{unfinished-computation} until the detector was
actually built, validated, and its output proved forced. The remaining seven are
argued from forced facts, not from framings --- but the distinction is exactly
what this section exists to keep visible.
